# Raid 2026-10-15: public review evidence

This single container replaces the unmerged per-PR evidence files. Each PR has a stable `pr-NNNN` anchor. Historical reports, attempt notes and their JSON payloads/hashes remain intact. Later metadata observations are appended as corrections, not silent rewrites. No provider-qualified pin is inferred from an observed model alias. Markdown link delimiters inside JSON strings use reversible Unicode escaping so raw historical examples are not treated as live links by a plain-text link scanner.

## Exact preserved source material

Each source section stores its exact UTF-8 text as a reversible JSON string, with the source path and SHA-256 above it. Decode the JSON string with `json.loads(payload)`, then encode the result as UTF-8 to recover the original bytes, including line endings and trailing spaces; verify those bytes against the stated SHA-256. Source files are never rewritten. The selected forge metadata at the end remains a JSON object, not an encoded source string. Forge metadata was read on 2026-10-15 after merge.

The prompt is rewritten as the new prompt, as authorized. This is not blanket substitution across historical ledgers.\n\n**Finding — stage-directory creation escapes the promised error path (verifiable, minor; confidence medium-high).** `apply_migration` calls `tempfile.mkdtemp(...)` before its `try` block. If creation fails, the raw `OSError` escapes instead of becoming `MigrationError`; the CLI catches only `MigrationError`, so the expected concise visible migration error becomes a traceback. Consider wrapping stage creation in the same error conversion (no rollback is needed because no mutation has started).\n\n**Nofollow:** No activation is expected in 1104. "# Astra advisory — PR 1429\n\nScope: HEAD `2a5e88e9adc12133b3f6020042ab2c3e5f318951`, feature base `e049a51dbc0dee6e3096beee93d7a7dd48bad4db`, worktree `/home/haduong/.agents/.claude/worktrees/t1106-raid`. Same family, not independent. Other-family perspective unavailable as supplied by the orchestrator; I made no outbound attempts. Advisory only, not a merge gate.\n\nRecommendation: the actual migration preserves the historical bytes and their meaning, but make one small test correction before relying on the stated exact-source provenance guarantee.\n\n## Required correction — historical source validation is absent\n\nHigh confidence. `tests/test_memory_v8_pilot.py:110–112` checks only that an exception source exists and its target belongs to the relocation table. It never loads `baseline:source`, compares the current source bytes, or proves that the historical source contained this link. The target side is correctly checked at lines 94–107; that does not authenticate the source side.\n\nDemonstration performed read-only: loaded the committed module with bytecode disabled, replaced `Path.read_text` in memory for the sole archival journal with its original text plus a false historical statement, then called both `verified_archival_links(root)` and `unresolved_links(root, source, allowed)`. Both accepted the altered source. No repository file changed. The real source is unchanged against the baseline; this is a demonstrated protection gap, not evidence of present corruption.\n\nStrongest remaining failure scenario: a later edit changes the exempted journal's historical account while retaining its old report link. The report hash remains valid, so this new provenance check still passes. An inventory row could similarly grant an existing source an exception without proving that source ever had that reference at the baseline.\n\nMinimal fix: for each declared exception load `git show baseline:source`, require the current source bytes to equal it, and verify that the normalized historical target appears among that original source's relative links. Add a negative control changing the source bytes while keeping the same link, and require rejection. No compatibility path or broader archive exemption is needed. This closes the launch brief's explicit “exact historical source blob + relocated report blob validation” requirement.\n\n## Smaller coverage gap\n\nHigh confidence, verifiable: `tests/test_memory_v8_pilot.py:213–216` enumerates memory and `docs/memory-v8/*.md` only. Consequently `docs/2026-10-02-memory-v8-runtime-smoke.md`, explicitly classified as current at lines 64–67, never reaches the integration link checker. Its current two migrated links resolve (checked directly). Add that one document to the enumeration to make its advertised strictness effective. This omission does not demonstrate a broken link in this revision.\n\n## Evidence and limits\n\nI inspected the actual prompt, correction journal, memory/docs diff and both changed test modules. The prompt's r1→r2 change explicitly distinguishes the new operational spelling from retained r1 historical reports. Six report renames are R100. I independently checked all 217 entries in `/tmp/t1106-preserved-before.json` against current SHA256 bytes, including relocated reports; all matched. The sole exempted journal also matches `baseline:source` byte for byte. I ran the new relocation verification helper against the real tree; its seven mappings passed. Current index, all topics, convention README and runtime-smoke links resolve physically using the committed parser.\n\nI independently reproduced the strict ledger count: 285 complete ledger rows, 244 distinct blobs, all accessible as Git blobs. That claim should stay qualified: a broader scan of all prose/native-note blob mentions finds 297 mentions and 248 distinct IDs, with three unavailable native-note IDs in the second/third historical reports. Those mentions are already present in byte-preserved source reports; this PR does not introduce their unavailability. nofollow: retain this limitation in the audit trail, not as a migration blocker.\n\nI did not rerun the full pytest/lint suites, exercise the already-merged migration implementation, verify remote publication/CI, or decrypt ciphertext. The supplied 1625/44/972/185 test counts are orchestrator evidence, not my executions. No source, Git, installation, or external-message mutation was performed; only this report artifact was written using atomic rename.\n\nAdvisory verdict: request the narrow source-provenance test correction; no demonstrated migration data loss.\n"

### Observation correction for PR 1429
